```css
    --export-html-btn-color: rgb(54, 90, 145);
    --export-xlsx-btn-color: rgb(58, 136, 47);
    --restore-btn-color: var(--primary-ui-color-darker);
    --backup-btn-color: var(--primary-ui-color);
    --light-colored-btn-color: rgb(207, 227, 227);
    --light-colored-btn-text: #0a5662;
    --dark-shadow: 1px 0 5px #0006;
    --light-shadow: 1px 0 5px #fff6;
    --th-border-color: #0000003b;
    --link-color: rgb(0, 45, 115);
    --detail-button-background: linear-gradient(180deg, hsl(210, 24%, 87%) 0%, hsl(210, 32%, 93%) 60%);
    --history-button-background: linear-gradient(180deg, hsl(210, 24%, 87%) 0%, hsl(210, 32%, 93%) 60%);
    --min-width: 1590px;
    --max-width: 2340px;
    --detail-border-color: #dfdfdf
}

body {
    font-family: "Inter Tight",Arial,sans-serif;
    margin: 8px;
    background-color: var(--background-color);
    color: var(--text-color);
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, #eaecf0 0%, var(--background-color) 13%, var(--background-color) 87%, #eaecf0 100%);
}

html.busyCursor body,html.busyCursor body * {
    cursor: wait
}

.loading-overlay {
    position: fixed;
    top: 58px;
    /*! left: 9px; */
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    width: calc(100% - 18px);
    height: calc(100% - 67px);
    /* backdrop-filter: blur(12px); */
    background: var(--modal-wrapper-background);
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
    transition: opacity .3s ease;
    transition-delay: .33s;
    border-radius: 8px;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 40px;
    min-width: var(--min-width);
    max-width: var(--max-width);
}
#html-export .loading-overlay {
    max-width: var(--max-width); 
}

html.busyCursor .loading-overlay {
    opacity: 1;
    pointer-events: all;
    z-index: 2;
}

.loading-spinner {
    width: 60px;
    height: 60px;
    border: 12px solid var(--primary-ui-color);
    border-top: 12px solid transparent;
    border-radius: 50%;
    animation: spin .75s linear infinite
}

.table-container {
    box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.35);
    border: 1px solid var(--primary-ui-color);
    overflow-y: auto;
    max-height: calc(100vh - 18px);
    position: relative;
    border-radius: 8px;
    min-width: var(--min-width);
    max-width: var(--max-width);
    margin: auto;
}
#html-export .table-container {
    max-width: var(--max-width); 
}

h3,th {
    position: sticky;
    top: 0;
    text-shadow: var(--dark-shadow)
}

h3 {
    z-index: 100;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 8px 0 8px 10px;
    background: var(--stats-primary-ui-color);
    color: #fefefe;
    font-weight: 400;
    width: calc(100% - 10px);
    background: linear-gradient(180deg,var(--primary-ui-color) 50%,var(--primary-ui-color-darker) 100%);

    /*! padding: 5px; */

}

th {
    vertical-align: bottom;
    background-color: var(--primary-ui-color);

    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 .75px var(--th-border-color);

}

table {

}

    border-radius: 3px;
    box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(64,64,64,.06);
    -webkit-user-select: none;
    -moz-user-select: none;
    -ms-user-select: none;
    user-select: none
}

.editable-status:hover, .editable-verify:hover, .pdf-status:hover, .editable-status.highlight-status:not(.cell-changed):hover {
    background-color: var(--light-colored-btn-color);
    transform: scale(1.1);
    box-shadow: 0 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.1)
}

.editable-status:active,.editable-verify:active,.pdf-status:active {
    transform: scale(.95);
    background-color: var(--light-colored-btn-color)
}

.detail-flex-container {
    max-height: 0;
    overflow: hidden;
    transition: max-height .4s ease-in-out,opacity .6s ease-in-out;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 720px;
    grid-template-rows: auto;
    gap: 9px;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    width: 100%;
    padding: 0 0 0 10px;
    margin: 0;
    opacity: 0;
}

.detail-row.expanded .detail-flex-container,
.history-row.expanded .history-flex-container {
    max-height: 800px;
    opacity: 1;
    /* transition: max-height 0s; */
    border-bottom: 1px solid #e0e0e0;
}

.detail-content {
    min-width: 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    padding: 4px 4px 0px 4px;
}

.detail-edit {
    min-width: 0;
    grid-column: 3/4;
    grid-row: 1/3
}

.detail-content p,.detail-edit p {
    text-align: justify;
    white-space: normal;
    margin-bottom: .5em;
    line-height: 1.3em;
    margin-block-start: .5em;
    margin-block-end: 0;
}

.detail-row>td, 
.history-row>td {
    padding: 0;
}

.detail-edit input[type=text] {
    font-family: inherit;
    font-size: 12pt;
    background: #ffffffa0;
    width: 100%;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    border: none;
}


.save-btn {
    background-color: var(--save-btn-color);
    width: 170px;
    font-weight: 700
}

#classify-all-btn, #classify-remaining-btn, #classify-impl-btn, #classify-misclassified-btn, #classify-consensus-btn, 
.classify-btn {
    background: var(--classify-btn-color);
}
#screen-remaining-btn, #classify-consensus-btn{
    width: 326px;
    background: linear-gradient(167deg, var(--classify-btn-color) 30%, var(--verify-btn-color)100%);
}
#verify-all-btn,#verify-remaining-btn,.verify-btn {
    background: var(--verify-btn-color)
}

#classify-all-btn strong,#verify-all-btn strong {
    text-decoration: underline
}

#export-html-btn {
    background: var(--export-html-btn-color)
}

#export-xlsx-btn {
    background: var(--export-xlsx-btn-color)
}

#backup-btn {
    background: var(--backup-btn-color)
}

#restore-btn {
    background: var(--restore-btn-color)
}

#stats-btn, #import-bibtex-btn {
    width: 200px;
    max-width: 200px;
    min-width: 200px;
    background: var(--stats-primary-ui-color)
}

#parça-tools-btn {
    background: var(--classify-btn-color)
}

#batchModal,#exportModal,#importModal {
    backdrop-filter: unset;
    background: unset;
    margin: auto;  
    max-width: var(--max-width);
    top: 50%;
    left: 50%;
    transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
}

#apply-serverside-filters,#html-export #stats-btn {
    width: 125px;
    max-width: 125px;
    min-width: 125px;
    font-size: 10pt;
    transition: .2s all ease-in-out;
    border-radius: 4px;
    cursor: pointer;
    box-shadow: 0 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.15);
    height: 40px;
    border: 1px solid #0002
}

#apply-serverside-filters,#html-export #about-btn,#html-export #stats-btn {
    background: var(--light-colored-btn-color);
    color: var(--light-colored-btn-text);
    text-shadow: var(--light-shadow)
}

#fullscreen-btn {
    background: 0 0;
    width: 28px;
    max-width: 28px;
    min-width: 28px;
    height: 36px;
    padding: 0;
    box-shadow: none;
    display: none
}

#about-btn,#html-export #about-btn {
    width: 30px;
    min-width: 30px;
    padding: 0
}

a {
    text-decoration: none;
    color: var(--link-color);
    font-weight: 400;
}

th.inferred-header {
    background: var(--inferred-ui-color);
    filter: contrast(1.0); /*Fix blurry transformed fonts on Firefox*/
}

th.inferred-header-2 {
    filter: contrast(1.1) saturate(.95) hue-rotate(-16deg);
}

th.inferred-header-3 {
    filter: contrast(1.15) saturate(.95) hue-rotate(-36deg)
}

#inferred-header-title {
    background: linear-gradient(90deg,var(--inferred-ui-color),var(--inferred-gradient-end-color))
}

#inferred-header-title div,.inferred-header:not(.rotate-header) div {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: row;
    justify-content: space-between;
    padding: 0 3px 0 0;
    align-items: end
}

.filter-item {
    white-space: nowrap
}

input[type=checkbox] {
    height: 12px;
    width: 12px;
    margin: 0;
    transform: scale(1.85,2) translate(1px,-1.5px);
    border-radius: 0;
    opacity: .5
}

input[type=checkbox]:checked,input[type=checkbox]:indeterminate {
    opacity: 1;
    filter: hue-rotate(-50deg) brightness(1.3)
}

.filter-item label {
    margin-bottom: 0
}

tr.filter-hidden {
    display: none
}

#counts-footer {
    position: sticky;
    bottom: 0;
    z-index: 998;
    border-radius: 6px
}

#counts-footer tr {
    background-color: var(--primary-ui-color);
    color: #fff;
    font-weight: 700
}

#counts-footer td {
    text-align: center;
    font-size: 8.5pt;
    padding: 9px 0
}

.trace-content {
    word-wrap: break-word;
    word-break: break-word;
    grid-row: 1/4;
    overflow-x: hidden;
    font-size: 10.5pt;
    overflow-y: auto;
    max-height: 720px;
    min-height: 720px;
}

.detail-evaluator-trace,.detail-verifier-trace {
    display: grid;
    min-width: 100%;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 170px;
    grid-template-rows: max-content max-content;
    align-items: end;
    border: 1px solid var( --detail-border-color);
    background-color: #aaaaaa0b;
    border-radius: 5px;
    margin: 0 0 8px;
    padding: 10px
}

#main-header-wrapper {
    padding: 0
}

.main-header {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: auto auto 1fr auto auto auto;
    grid-template-rows: auto;
    gap: 4px;
    align-items: center;
    background-color: #e0e6ed;
    box-shadow: 0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.1);
    min-width: var(--min-width);
    max-width: var(--max-width);
    height: 48px;
    background: var(--detail-button-background);
    border-radius: 8px 8px 0 0;
    padding: 0 4px;
}

#html-export .main-header {
    max-width: var(--max-width); 
}
.header-title h2 {
    margin: 0;
    color: #2c3e50;
    font-size: 1.5em
}

.header-filters {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 15px;
    align-items: center;
    color: #eee;
    font-weight: 400;
    justify-content: space-between
}

.filter-item {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    padding: 5px;
    font-size: 10.5pt
}

.menu-message {
    font-size: 10pt;
    padding: 4px
}

#batch-actions,#export-actions,#import-actions {
    z-index: 1000;
    overflow: auto;
    opacity: 1;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    position: fixed;
    /* backdrop-filter: blur(16px); 
    background: #f5f7fad9;*/
    background: var(--detail-button-background);
    perspective: 100vh;
    flex-direction: column;
    padding: 10px;
    gap: 16px;
    border-radius: 8px;
    border: 1px solid #444;
    box-shadow: 0 48px 96px 32px rgba(64,64,64,.5);
    box-sizing: border-box;
    transition: opacity .3s ease-in-out,transform .3s ease-out;
    width: 358px;
    text-align: justify
}

#batch-actions {
    top: 56px;
    right: 260px;
    transform: scale3d(.75,.66,1) translateY(-116px) translateX(40px);
}

#export-actions,#import-actions {
    top: 56px;
    left: 10px;
    transform: scale3d(.75,.66,1) translateY(-66px) translateX(-40px)
}

#export-actions {
    left: 210px;
}

.header-controls {
    margin: 0;
}

.detail-edit {
    /*! display: grid; */
    height: 100%;
    padding: 8px 8px 8px 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
}

.detail-edit form {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr 1fr 120px 120px;
    grid-template-rows: auto auto 1fr auto;
    gap: 4px;
    height: calc(100% - 0px);
}

.detail-edit form>label:nth-of-type(1) {
    grid-column: 1/3;
    grid-row: 1/2
}

.detail-edit form>label:nth-of-type(2) {
    grid-column: 3/7;
    grid-row: 1/2
}

.detail-edit form>label:nth-of-type(3) {
    grid-column: 1/5;
    grid-row: 2/3
}

.detail-edit form>label:nth-of-type(4) {
    grid-column: 5/6;
    grid-row: 2/3
}

.detail-edit form>label:nth-of-type(5) {
    grid-column: 6/7;
    grid-row: 2/3
}

#html-export .detail-edit form {
    height: calc(100% - 2px)
}

.detail-edit form>label:nth-of-type(6) {
    grid-column: 1/-1;
    grid-row: 3/4;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column
}

.detail-edit form textarea.editable {
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    min-height: 150px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    display: block;
    resize: none;
    background: #ffffffa0;
    font-family: consolas,monospace;
    font-size: .9em;
    line-height: 1.25em;
    border: none;
}

.detail-edit form>.row-actions {
    grid-column: 1/-1;
    grid-row: 4/5;
    text-align: left;
    display: flex;
    align-content: space-around;
    justify-content: space-between;
}

.detail-edit form textarea.editable:focus, .detail-edit form input.editable:focus {
   outline: none;
   border: none;
}
.detail-edit form label:focus-within {
   border: 1px solid #338592;
}

.number-cell {
    text-align: center
}

.ontop-header {
    z-index: 100
}

.modal-content,.modal-small-content {
    background: linear-gradient(180deg,var(--stats-primary-ui-color) 0%,var(--stats-primary-ui-color) 20px,white 20px);
    /* background: linear-gradient(180deg,var(--stats-primary-ui-color) 0%,var(--stats-primary-ui-color) 32px, #f8fbfef0 32px); */
    border-radius: 8px;
    box-shadow: 0 16px 96px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.66);
    position: relative;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    overflow: hidden;
    /* transform: scale3d(.94,.94,1) rotate3d(2,1,0,2.5deg) translateY(-20px); */
    transform: scale(0.94);
    transition: transform .3s ease-out;
    display: grid
}

.modal-content {
    width: calc(100vw - 60px);
    min-width: calc(var(--min-width) + 0px);
    height: calc(100vh - 12px);
    grid-template-columns: 640px 1fr;
}


.modal-small-content {
    /* why? This breaks the about modal when the configured amount of columns is small*/
    /*width: calc(var(--min-width) - 100px); */
    width: 1530px;
    min-width: 1530px;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 320px;
    /*! min-width: calc(var(--min-width) - 0); */
}

.modal-small-section h4,.modal-small-section p,.modal-small-section ul {
    padding: 0 18px
}

.close {
    color: #eee;
    float: right;
    font-size: 28px;
    font-weight: 700;
    position: absolute;
    right: 11px;
    top: -2px;
    cursor: pointer;
    z-index: 1001;
    text-shadow: var(--dark-shadow)
}

.close:focus,.close:hover {
    color: #fff;
    text-decoration: none
}

.stats-section ul {
    list-style-type: none;
    padding-left: 0;
    margin: 0;
    overflow: hidden
}

.stats-section li {
    padding: 4px 3px;
    font-size: 9pt
}

.stats-section li span.count {
    font-weight: 700;
    min-width: 40px;
    display: inline-block;
    text-align: center
}

.stats-section p {
    padding: 0 10px;
    font-size: 10.5pt
}

.stats-list-multicolumn-short {
    column-count: 3;
    padding: 0 10px
}

.stats-list-multicolumn-2,.stats-list-multicolumn-short {
    column-gap: 0;
    break-inside: avoid-column
}

.stats-list-multicolumn li {
    break-inside: avoid
}

.modal {
    z-index: 1000;
    overflow: auto;
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
    transition: opacity .25s ease-in-out;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    background: var(--modal-wrapper-background);
    /* backdrop-filter: blur(12px); */
    perspective: 100vh
}

.modal p {
    text-align: justify
}

#help-text{
    column-count: 2;
    column-gap: 0px;
    padding: 0px 10px 20px 0px;
}

.modal.modal-active {
    opacity: 1;
    pointer-events: auto;
    transition-delay: .1s
}

.modal.modal-active #batch-actions,
.modal.modal-active #export-actions,
.modal.modal-active #import-actions,
.modal.modal-active .modal-content,
.modal.modal-active .modal-small-content {
    transform: none;
    transition-delay: .02s
}

.stats-section {
    overflow-y: hidden;
    overflow-x: hidden
}

.stats-lists {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-rows: 2fr 328px 1fr 191px;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    min-height: 0
}

.stats-lists .stats-section {
    overflow-y: scroll;
    overflow-x: hidden;
    border-right: 2px solid var(--stats-primary-ui-color)
}

.stats-charts {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr 1fr 1fr 1fr 1fr 1fr;
    grid-template-rows: calc(26vh - 6px) calc(36vh - 6px) calc(20vh) calc(18vh)
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(1) {
 /*Publ. Type Distribution */
    grid-row: 4/5;
    grid-column: 3/4;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(2) {
 /* Survey vs Primary Distribution */
    grid-row: 4/5;
    grid-column: 4/5;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(3) {
 /* Techniques Distribution */
    grid-row: 1/2;
    grid-column: 1/3;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(4) {
 /* Features Distribution */
    grid-row: 2/3;
    grid-column: 1/3;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(5) {
 /*Publ.type per year*/
    grid-row: 3/4;
    grid-column: 1/5;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(6) {
 /*Survey vs Primary Papers per Year*/
    grid-row: 4/3;
    grid-column: 5/9;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(7) {
 /*Techniques per Year */
    grid-row: 1/2;
    grid-column: 3/9;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(8) {
 /*Features per Year */
    grid-row: 2/3;
    grid-column: 3/9;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(9) {
 /*relevance */
    grid-row: 4/5;
    grid-column: 9/7;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(10) {
 /*verifier */
    grid-row: 4/5;
    grid-column: 5/7;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(11) {
 /* smt */
    grid-row: 4/5;
    grid-column: 2/3;
}

.stats-charts .stats-section:nth-child(12) {
 /* scope */
    grid-row: 4/5;
    grid-column: 1/2;
}

.search-item-btn {
    background: 0 0;
    border: 0;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: .9em;
    border-radius: 3px;
    padding: 1px 2px;
    transform: scale(1.5) translate(-4px,-.5px);
    opacity: .5;
    transition: .5s opacity ease;
    font-family: "Segoe UI Emoji Windows 11", "Twemoji Mozilla";
}

.search-item-btn:hover {
    background-color: #f0f0f0;
    opacity: 1
}

li {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 4px
}

.stats-list-multicolumn-2 li .count,.stats-list-multicolumn-short li .count {
    flex-shrink: 0
}

.stats-list-multicolumn-2 li .name,.stats-list-multicolumn-short li .name {
    flex-grow: 1;
    word-break: break-word
}

.stats-list-multicolumn-2 li .search-item-btn,.stats-list-multicolumn-short li .search-item-btn {
    flex-shrink: 0
}

.search-container {
    position: relative;
    display: inline-block;
    width: 100%
}

.clear-btn {
    position: absolute;
    right: 3px;
    top: 50%;
    transform: translateY(-50%);
    border-radius: 0 6px 6px 0;
    border: 0;
    color: #fff;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 24px;
    width: 32px;
    height: 40px;
    display: none;
    transition: opacity .2s;
    padding: 0;
    background: var(--primary-ui-color)
}

#search-input:not(:placeholder-shown)+.clear-btn {
    display: inline
}

.clear-btn:hover {
    color: #eee
}

#metricsTableStatsList {
    min-width: 0;
    font-size: 10pt;
    background: #0000;
    text-align: right
}

#metricsTableStatsList td {
    padding: 7px
}

#keywordCloud span {
    display: inline-block;
    margin: 4px 6px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: transform .15s
}

#keywordCloud span:hover {
    transform: scale(1.15)
}

.clickable-item {
    border-radius: 5px;
    padding: 4px 6px;
    margin: 0;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: .85em;
    transition: .33s all ease
}

.clickable-item:hover {
    background-color: var(--light-colored-btn-color);
    box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.1);
    opacity: 1;
    color: var(--light-colored-btn-text);
    text-shadow: var(--light-shadow)
}

.clickable-authors,.clickable-keywords {
    text-align: justify
}

.id-section {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: min-content 1fr min-content min-content min-content;
    border: 1px solid var( --detail-border-color);
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 6px;
    margin: 6px 0;
    background-color: #aaaaaa0b;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px
}

.bibtex-section {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr min-content;
    grid-template-rows: min-content 1fr
}

.bibtex-section,.searchable-section {
    border: 1px solid var( --detail-border-color);
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 6px;
    margin: 6px 0 9px;
    background-color: #aaaaaa0b;
    align-items: center;
}

.bibtex-pre {
    overflow: scroll;
    grid-column: 1/3;
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 7pt;
}

.bibtex-copy-btn,.id-copy-btn, .id-citen-btn, .id-cite-btn, .tabularx-btn, #longtable-btn {
    background-color: var(--light-colored-btn-color);
    color: var(--light-colored-btn-text);
    border-radius: 4px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all .33s ease;
    box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(64,64,64,.1);
    text-shadow: var(--light-shadow);
    border: 1px solid #0002;
    padding: 6.5px;
    width: 66px;
    font-size: 9pt;
}
.tabularx-btn, #longtable-btn {
    padding: 4px;
    width: 66px;
    font-size: 9pt;
}

.about-emoji-table{
    font-size: 1.75em;
    padding: 4px;
    font-family: "Segoe UI Emoji Windows 11", "Twemoji Mozilla"
}

.emoji-column {
    font-family: "Segoe UI Emoji Windows 11", "Twemoji Mozilla"
}
.about-emoji-text{
    padding-left: 9px; 
    padding-right: 9px; 
    display: inline-block;
    transform: scale(1.25);
    font-family: "Segoe UI Emoji Windows 11", "Twemoji Mozilla"
}


@media screen and (max-width:1880px) {
    body {
        margin: 0
    }

    .table-container {
        max-height: calc(100vh);
        border-radius: 0;
        background: var(--primary-ui-color);
        border: 0
    }

    .loading-overlay {
        left: 0;
        width: calc(100%);
        height: calc(100%);
        border-radius: 0
    }

    .main-header {
        border-radius: 0
    }

    #batch-actions {
        top: 48px;
        right: 252px;
    }

    #import-actions {
        top: 48px;
        left: 6px
    }

    #export-actions {
        top: 48px;
        left: 206px;
    }

    .modal-content {
        width: calc(100vw);
        min-width: calc(var(--min-width) + 0px);
        height: calc(100vh);
        border-radius: 0;
    }
    .modal-content,.modal-small-content  {
        background: linear-gradient(180deg,var(--stats-primary-ui-color) 0%,var(--stats-primary-ui-color) 20px,white 20px);
        backdrop-filter: none !important;
    }
}

::-webkit-scrollbar {
    width: 0;
    height: 0
}


/* Individual log entry container */
.log-entry {
  border: 1px solid #ddd;
  padding: 10px;
}

/* Header section of a log entry */
.log-header {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between; /* Distribute items evenly */
  align-items: center; /* Vertically align items */
  border-bottom: 1px dashed #ccc; /* Separator line */
  padding-bottom: 4px;
  margin-bottom: 4px;
  font-size: 0.9em; /* Slightly smaller text for header */
}

.log-timestamp {
  font-weight: bold;
  color: #555;
}

.log-type {
  font-weight: bold;
  /* Color coding based on type can be added here if desired */
  /* Example: */
  /* &.classifier { color: blue; } */
  /* &.verifier { color: orange; } */
  /* &.user { color: green; } */
}

.log-model {
  color: #666;
}

.valid-indicator.valid {
  color: green;
}

.valid-indicator.invalid {
  color: red;
}

/* Trace content section */
.log-trace pre {
  white-space: pre-wrap; /* Wrap long lines */
  word-break: break-word; /* Break words if necessary */
  margin: 0; /* Remove default margins from pre tag */
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 9.5pt; /* Subtle background for trace */
  text-align: justify; /* Visual indicator */
  overflow-x: hidden; /* Scroll horizontally if needed */
}

/* Raw output details section (if included) */
.log-output-details pre {
  white-space: pre-wrap;
  word-break: break-word;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 4px;
  background-color: #f0f0f0;
  border-left: 2px solid #aaa;
  overflow-x: auto;
  max-height: 150px; /* Limit height */
  overflow-y: auto; /* Scroll vertically if needed */
}

.log-entry.log-type-consensus_round_start,
.log-entry.log-type-consensus_round_end {
  background-color: #e6ffe6; /* Light green for consensus */
}

/* Add more type-specific colors as needed */


.history-table{
  table-layout: fixed;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  min-width: 0px;
  max-width: 0px;
  border: 1px solid var( --detail-border-color);
/*   background-color: #a0c8aa0f; */
  background-color: #aaaaaa0b;
}





/* Log entry type-specific background colors only */
.log-entry.log-type-classifier, .log-entry.log-type-averaged_llm {
    background-color: #f6f8ff;
}

.log-entry.log-type-verifier {
    background-color: #fef8ff;
}

.log-entry.log-type-consensus {
    background-color: #e6ffe6;
}

.log-entry.log-type-user {
    background-color: #fafafa;
}

.log-entry.log-type-screener {
    background-color: #fff8e1; /* Light yellow for triage */
}

.log-verification-summary {
    margin-top: 8px;
    padding: 6px;
    background-color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03);
    border-radius: 4px;
    font-size: 9.5pt;
}

.history-row table td {
    /*! padding: 9px 6px; */ /* Match main table td padding */
    height: 58px;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    vertical-align: middle;
    overflow: hidden;
    box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(64,64,64,.04);
}

.history-row table td:not(.cell-changed) {
    opacity: 1;
}
.cell-changed {
    background: rgba(207, 227, 227, 0.75);
}

.history-info{
    grid-row: 2/3;
    grid-column: 2/3;
    margin: 8px;
    color: var(--light-colored-btn-text);
    border: 1px solid var( --detail-border-color);
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 6px;
    background-color: #aaaaaa0b;
    align-items: center;
    display: grid;
    justify-content: center;
}

.history-row .row-actions{
    grid-row: 3/4;
    grid-column: 2/3;
    /*! align-self:end; */
    /*! justify-self: end; */
    padding: 0px 6px 8px 8px;
    text-align: left;
    display: flex;
    align-content: space-around;
    justify-content: space-between;
}










.history-tab-btn {
      /* Each tab takes 25% height */
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 11pt;
    font-weight: 400;
    color: var(--text-color);
    transition: all 0.2s ease;
    /*! text-align: center; */
    /*! writing-mode: vertical-rl; */
    /*! text-orientation: mixed; */
    /*! transform: rotate(180deg); */
    padding: 0;
    margin: 0;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: stretch;
    /*! min-width: 27px; */
    /*! max-width: 27px; */
    border: none;
    border-right: 1px solid var(--detail-border-color);
}

/* Conflict warning - replaces emoji entirely */
.conflict-warning {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 1.25em;
}


.history-flex-container {
    max-height: 0;
    overflow: hidden;
    transition: max-height .4s ease-in-out,opacity .6s ease-in-out;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 27px 1fr;
    grid-template-rows: min-content 1fr min-content;
    gap: 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    width: 100%;
    margin: 0;
    opacity: 0;
}

.history-tab-panel.active {
    /*! max-height: 0; */
    overflow: hidden;
    transition: max-height .4s ease-in-out,opacity .6s ease-in-out;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr var(--history-table-width);
    grid-template-rows: min-content 1fr min-content;
    gap: 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    width: 100%;
    margin: 0;
    /*! opacity: 0; */
}




/* History Row Tabs - Vertical Navigation (27px wide, aligned with PDF column) */
.history-tabs-nav {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-rows: 180px 180px 180px 180px;
    gap: 1px;
    padding: 0;
    background: #f5f7fa;
    border-right: 1px solid var(--detail-border-color);
    width: 27px;
    min-width: 27px;
    max-width: 27px;
    height: 720px;
    min-height: 720px;
}


.history-tab-btn.active {
    background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--primary-ui-color) 0%, var(--primary-ui-color-darker) 100%);
    color: #fff;
    border: none;
    text-shadow: var(--dark-shadow);
}

.history-tab-btn span{
    width: 40px;
}



/* History Row Tabs - Vertical Navigation (27px wide, aligned with PDF column) */
.history-tabs-nav {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-rows: 180px 180px 180px 180px;
    gap: 1px;
    padding: 0;
    background: #f5f7fa;
    border-right: 1px solid var(--detail-border-color);
    width: 27px;
    min-width: 27px;
    max-width: 27px;
    height: 720px;
    min-height: 720px;
}

.history-tab-btn {
    /* Each tab takes 25% height */
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 10.5pt;
    font-weight: 400;
    color: var(--text-color);
    transition: all 0.2s ease;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 0;
    margin: 0;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    min-width: 27px;
    max-width: 27px;
    border: none;
    border-right: 1px solid var(--detail-border-color);
    background: linear-gradient(180deg, hsl(210, 24%, 87%) 0%, hsl(210, 32%, 93%) 60%);
    filter: contrast(1);
    font-family: Inter Tight;
}

/* Use same rotation technique as table headers for proper font rendering */
.history-tab-btn > span {
    transform: rotate(-90deg);
    width: 27px;
    display: block;
    text-align: left;
    white-space: nowrap;
    filter: contrast(1);
}

.history-tab-btn.active {
    background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--primary-ui-color) 0%, var(--primary-ui-color-darker) 100%);
    color: #fff;
    border: none;
    text-shadow: var(--dark-shadow);
}



#history-table-wrapper {
    max-height: 540px;
    overflow: scroll;
}


/* In static/style.css */
.invalid-entry {
    background: #ffeeee !important;
}
.invalid-entry td {
    color: #cc0000;
    font-style: italic;
}


.history-tabs-content {
    padding: 0;
    margin: 0;
}

.history-tab-panel:not(.active) {
    display: none;
    overflow: hidden;
    height: 0;
}

/* Certainty opacity classes - apply to emoji-content span ONLY */
.certainty-60 .emoji-content {
    opacity: 0.33;
}

.certainty-80 .emoji-content {
    opacity: 0.66;
}

/* Conflict warning - replaces emoji entirely */
.conflict-warning {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 1em;
}

/* Media query for high device pixel ratio (HiDPI) screens */
/* This targets the *physical* pixel density of the screen */
@media screen and (min-resolution: 2dppx) {   /* Minimum 2 device pixels per CSS pixel */

    /* Slows down the spin animation */
    .loading-spinner {
        animation: spin 1.5s linear infinite !important;
    }

    /* Overrides max-height/opacity transitions */
    .detail-flex-container {
        transition: none !important;
    }
    
    /* Target potential hover effects causing frequent redraws */
    .editable-status:hover,
    .editable-verify:hover,
    .pdf-status:hover,
    .action-btn:hover,
    .toggle-btn:hover {
        transform: none !important;
    }
    .detail-flex-container, .detail-row.expanded .detail-flex-container{
        transition: none;
    }
    /* The effects below are now disabled globally instead: */
    /* .modal-content,.modal-small-content  {
        background: linear-gradient(180deg,var(--stats-primary-ui-color) 0%,var(--stats-primary-ui-color) 20px,white 20px);
        backdrop-filter: none !important;
    } */


    /* Target the modal backdrop filter specifically */
    /* .loading-overlay, .modal {
        backdrop-filter: none !important;
        background: #eeee
    } */

    /* Removes initial modal 3D scaling/rotation */
    /* ,.modal-content,
    .modal-small-content
    #batch-actions,
    #import-actions,
    #export-actions
    {
        transform: scale(0.9);
    }  */

}


/* ==========================================================================
   Agreement Report: batch-modal entry point + deep-link row highlight
   ========================================================================== */
#agreement-report-btn,
#queue-logs-btn {
    display: inline-block;
    /*! background-color: var(--light-colored-btn-color); */
    /*! color: var(--light-colored-btn-text); */
    /*! border-radius: 4px; */
    /*! cursor: pointer; */
    /*! transition: all .33s ease; */
    /*! box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(64, 64, 64, .1); */
    /*! text-shadow: var(--light-shadow); */
    /*! border: 1px solid #0002; */
    padding: 9px 16px;
    font-size: 10pt;
    width: 100%;
    box-sizing: border-box;
}

#agreement-report-btn:hover,
#queue-logs-btn:hover {
    filter: saturate(200%) contrast(110%);
}

#agreement-report-btn:active,
#queue-logs-btn:active {
    transform: translateY(1px);
    filter: contrast(1.2) brightness(.8);
    transition: all .1s ease;
}

/* Pulsing highlight applied by focus_paper.js when an outlier link in the
   agreement report focuses a paper row in this window. */
@keyframes focus-pulse {
    0%, 100% { background-color: rgba(255, 213, 79, 0); }
    50%      { background-color: rgba(255, 213, 79, 0.65); }
}
tr.focus-highlight > td {
    animation: focus-pulse 1.2s ease-in-out 2;
}


/* Agent Trace Review (free-form meta-analysis) entry */
.log-entry.log-type-trace_review {
    background-color: #e6f3f1;
    border-left: 4px solid #10a9bd;
}
.trace-review-report pre {
    white-space: pre-wrap;
    word-break: break-word;
    margin: 0;
    font-family: inherit;
    font-size: 10pt;
    line-height: 1.4;
    text-align: justify;
    overflow-x: hidden;
}
.trace-review-thinking {
    margin-top: 6px;
    border-top: 1px dashed #ccc;
    padding-top: 4px;
}
.trace-review-thinking summary {
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 9pt;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--primary-ui-color);
    user-select: none;
}
.trace-review-thinking[open] summary {
    margin-bottom: 4px;
}
.trace-review-btn {
    width: 180px
}

```

# web\templates\detail_row.html
```html
{#<!-- templates/detail_row.html -->#}
{% from 'shared/detail_form.html' import render_detail_form %}

{% block detail_row_content %}
<div class="detail-flex-container">
  <div class="detail-content detail-abstract">
    <div class="id-section">
      <strong>ID:</strong> {{ paper.id }}
      <button type="button" class="id-cite-btn" onclick="copyPaperId('{{ paper.id }}', this, 'cite')" title="Copy as \cite{}"><em>\cite</em></button>
      <button type="button" class="id-citen-btn" onclick="copyPaperId('{{ paper.id }}', this, 'citen')" title="Copy as \citeN{}"><em>\citeN</em></button>
      <button type="button" class="id-copy-btn" onclick="copyPaperId('{{ paper.id }}', this, 'raw')" title="Copy as plain text">Copy</button>
    </div>
    <p><strong>Abstract:</strong> {{ paper.abstract }}</p>
  </div>

  <div class="detail-content detail-metadata">
    <div class="bibtex-section">
      <strong>BibTeX Citation:</strong>
      <button type="button" class="bibtex-copy-btn" onclick="copyBibtex(`{{ paper | bibtex }}`, this)">Copy</button>
      <pre class="bibtex-pre">{{ paper | bibtex }}</pre>
    </div>
    <div class="searchable-section">
      <span style="font-size:0.8em; color: var(--light-colored-btn-text);">Click on any keyword or author below to <strong>search</strong> for it:</span>
      <p><strong>Keywords:</strong>
        <span class="clickable-keywords">
          {% if paper.keywords %}
            {% for keyword in paper.keywords.split(';') %}
              {% if keyword.strip() %}<span class="clickable-item" data-search-field="keywords" data-search-term="{{ keyword.strip() }}">{{ keyword.strip() }};</span>{% endif %}
            {% endfor %}
          {% endif %}
        </span>
      </p>
      <p><strong>Authors:</strong>
        <span class="clickable-authors">
          {% if paper.authors %}
            {% for author in paper.authors.split(';') %}
              {% if author.strip() %}<span class="clickable-item" data-search-field="authors" data-search-term="{{ author.strip() }}">{{ author.strip() }};</span>{% endif %}
            {% endfor %}
          {% endif %}
        </span>
      </p>
    </div>
    <p>
      <strong>DOI:</strong> {% if paper.doi %}<a href="https://doi.org/{{ paper.doi }}" target="_blank">{{ paper.doi }}</a>{% endif %}<br>
      <strong>ISSN:</strong> {{ paper.issn or 'None' }}
      <strong> — Page Range/Start:</strong> {{ paper.pages }}
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="edit-section detail-edit">
    {{ render_detail_form(paper, domain_config, editable=true) }}
  </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

# web\templates\history_row.html
```html
{#<!-- templates/history_row.html -->#}
{% from 'shared/history_table.html' import render_history_tab %}

{% block history_row_content %}
{% set ns = namespace(total_dynamic=0) %}
{% for group in domain_config.groups %}
  {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
    {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + 1 %}
  {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
    {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + (group.fields | length) %}
  {% endif %}
{% endfor %}
{% set history_table_width = 378 + ns.total_dynamic * 27 %}

<div class="history-flex-container" style="--history-table-width: {{ history_table_width }}px;">
  <div class="history-tabs-nav">
    <button class="history-tab-btn active" data-tab="main" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}"><span>Main <span class="arrow">▼</span></span></button>
    <button class="history-tab-btn" data-tab="set1" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}"><span>Set 1 <span class="arrow">▼</span></span></button>
    <button class="history-tab-btn" data-tab="set2" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}"><span>Set 2 <span class="arrow">▼</span></span></button>
    <button class="history-tab-btn" data-tab="set3" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}"><span>Set 3 <span class="arrow">▼</span></span></button>
  </div>
  <div class="history-tabs-content">
    {{ render_history_tab(paper, none, 'main', domain_config, show_certainty=true, show_actions=true, is_lite_export=false) }}
    {{ render_history_tab(paper, 1, 'set1', domain_config, show_certainty=false, show_actions=false, is_lite_export=false) }}
    {{ render_history_tab(paper, 2, 'set2', domain_config, show_certainty=false, show_actions=false, is_lite_export=false) }}
    {{ render_history_tab(paper, 3, 'set3', domain_config, show_certainty=false, show_actions=false, is_lite_export=false) }}
  </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

# web\templates\index.html
```html
{#<!-- templates/index.html -->#}
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en" class="busyCursor">
<head>
    <title>ResearchParsa - {{ domain_config.domain_name }}</title>    
    <link rel="icon" href="data:image/svg+xml,<svg xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22 viewBox=%220 0 100 100%22><text y=%22.9em%22 font-size=%2290%22>📊</text></svg>">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="{{ url_for('static', filename='css/fonts.css') }}">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="{{ url_for('static', filename='css/style.css') }}">

    {#<!-- Inject Dynamic Theme Overrides -->#}
    {% if domain_config.theme_css %}
    <style>
    {{ domain_config.theme_css | safe }}
    </style>
    {% endif %}
    
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='libs/chart.min.js') }}"></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='libs/chartjs-plugin-datalabels.min.js') }}"></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='libs/d3.min.js') }}"></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='libs/d3-cloud.min.js') }}"></script>
        
    <script>
        window.APP_CONFIG = {{ domain_config | tojson | safe }};
    </script>
    <script>
        window.FOCUS_PAPER_ID = {{ focus_paper | tojson }};
    </script>
    
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/core/papers_store.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/core/table_renderer.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/core/virtual_scroll.js') }}" defer></script>

    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/stats/stats_core.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/stats/stats_generic.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/stats/stats_charts.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/stats/stats_domain.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/stats/stats_latex.js') }}" defer></script> 

    <!-- Replace the single comms.js line with: -->
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/comms/comms_rendering.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/comms/comms_views.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/comms/comms_save.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/comms/comms_batch.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/comms/comms_files.js') }}" defer></script>

    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/filtering/filtering_state.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/filtering/filtering_engine.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/filtering/filtering_actions.js') }}" defer></script>
    <script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/filtering/filtering_init.js') }}" defer></script>
    
</head>
<body>
<input type="file" id="bibtex-file-input" accept=".bib,.csv" style="display: none;">
<div class="table-container" id="papers-table-container">
    <table id="papersTable" style="table-layout: fixed;">

        {% set ns = namespace(total_dynamic=0) %}
        {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
            {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + 1 %}
            {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + (group.fields | length) %}
            {% endif %}
        {% endfor %}
        {% set inferred_colspan = 2 + ns.total_dynamic %}
        {% set total_cols = 6 + inferred_colspan + 9 %}

        <colgroup>
            {#<!-- 1. LEADING FIXED (6) -->#}
            <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;"> {#<!-- PDF -->#}
            <col style="width: 66%"> {#<!-- Title -->#}
            <col style="width: 40px; min-width: 40px; max-width:40px;"> {#<!-- Year -->#}
            <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Pages -->#}
            <col style="width: 34%"> {#<!-- Journal -->#}
            <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Type -->#}
            
            {#<!-- 2. UNIVERSAL INFERRED (2) -->#}
            <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Off-topic -->#}
            <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Relevance -->#}

            {#<!-- 3. DYNAMIC DOMAIN-SPECIFIC -->#}
            {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
                {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                    <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
                {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                    {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                        <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
                    {% endfor %}
                {% endif %}
            {% endfor %}

            {#<!-- 4. TRAILING FIXED (9) -->#}
            <col style="width: 56px; min-width: 56px; max-width: 56px;"> {#<!-- Last Changed -->#}
            <col style="width: 40px; min-width: 40px; max-width: 40px;"> {#<!-- Changed By -->#}
            <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Verified -->#}
            <col style="width: 32px; min-width: 32px; max-width: 32px;"> {#<!-- Accr. Score -->#}
            <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Verified By -->#}
            <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;"> {#<!-- User Changes -->#}
            <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Commented -->#}
            <col style="width: 44px; min-width: 44px; max-width: 44px;"> {#<!-- History -->#}
            <col style="width: 44px; min-width: 44px; max-width: 44px;"> {#<!-- Details -->#}

            {#<!-- 5. HIDDEN METADATA (5) -->#}
            <col style="display:none"><col style="display:none"><col style="display:none"><col style="display:none"><col style="display:none">
        </colgroup>

        <thead>
            {#<!-- ROW 1: Main Header Wrapper -->#}
            <tr>
                <th id="main-header-wrapper" colspan="{{ total_cols }}">
                    <div class="main-header">
                        <div class="header-controls">
                            <button class="action-btn" id="import-btn" style="display:none">Import / Restore</button>
                            <button class="action-btn" id="import-bibtex-btn" title="Supported: Scopus, ACM, Zotero BibTeX, IEEE Xplore CSV">Import <strong>BibTeX / CSV</strong></button>
                        </div>
                        <div class="header-controls">
                            <button class="action-btn" id="export-btn"><strong>Export</strong> & Backup</button>
                        </div>
                        <div class="header-branding"><span>Research</span><span style="font-weight: 600; color: var(--primary-ui-color);">Parsa</span> - <span style="font-size: 0.667em;">{{ domain_config.domain_name }}</span></div>
                        <div class="header-controls">
                            <button class="action-btn" id="parça-tools-btn" ><strong>Batch</strong> Tools</button>
                        </div>
                        <div class="header-controls">
                            <button class="action-btn" id="stats-btn">View <strong>Statistics</strong> <span style="color:#fff8">(F4)</span></button>
                        </div>
                        <div class="header-controls">
                            <button class="action-btn" id="about-btn" title="(F1) About / Help" ><span>?</span></button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </th>
                <th style="display:none" colspan="5"></th>
            </tr>

            {#<!-- ROW 2: Server Filters | Inferred Data Wrapper | Search -->#}
            <tr>
                <th class="ontop-header" colspan="6" rowspan="2">
                    <div class="header-filters">
                        <div class="filter-item">
                            <label for="min-page-count">Min. page count ≥</label>
                            <input type="number" id="min-page-count" value="{{ min_page_count_value }}" min="0" max="9">
                        </div>
                        <div class="filter-item">
{#<!-- This should be updated to become dynamically limited according to existing data in the current DB: -->#}
                            <label for="year-from">Year:</label>
                            <input type="number" id="year-from" value="{{ year_from_value }}" min="1800" max="2038">
                            <label for="year-to">to</label>
                            <input type="number" id="year-to" value="{{ year_to_value }}" min="1800" max="2038">
                        </div>
                        <button class="action-btn" id="apply-serverside-filters" style="opacity:0; pointer-events: none;"><span>Apply ↵</span></button>
                    </div>
                </th>
                
                <th class="inferred-header" id="inferred-header-title" colspan="{{ inferred_colspan }}">
                    <div>
                        <span>Inferred data </span>
                        <div style="margin:0; padding:0">
                            <!-- Potential misclassifications: -->
                            <input type="checkbox" title="Enable to only show papers marked as misclassified by the verifier" id="hide-approved-checkbox" style="margin: 0 0 0 8px;">
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </th>

                <th class="ontop-header" colspan="9" rowspan="2" >
                    <div class="search-container">
                        <input type="search" id="search-input" placeholder="(F3) Type to search all fields..." value="{{ search_query_value }}">
                        <button type="button" id="clear-search-btn" class="clear-btn" title="Clear search">&times;</button>
                    </div>
                </th>
                <th style="display:none" colspan="5"></th>
            </tr>

            {#<!-- ROW 3: Group Headers & Filter Checkboxes -->#}
            <tr>
                {#<!-- Universal Inferred Checkboxes -->#}
                <th class="inferred-header ontop-header" colspan="1">
                    <div>
                        <input type="checkbox" title="Enable to Hide Offtopic papers" id="hide-offtopic-checkbox" {% if hide_offtopic == true %}checked{% endif %}>
                    </div>
                </th>
                <th class="inferred-header ontop-header" colspan="1"></th> {#<!-- Empty space above Relevance -->#}

                {#<!-- Dynamic Domain-Specific Group Headers -->#}
                {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
                    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                    <th class="inferred-header {{ group.header_class | default('') }} ontop-header" colspan="1">
                        <div>
                            {% if group.friendly_name %}<span>{{ group.friendly_name }}</span>{% endif %}
                            <input type="checkbox" class="tri-state-checkbox" data-filter-group="{{ group.name }}" title="Enable to only show {{ group.label }}">
                        </div>
                    </th>
                    {% elif group.filter_type == 'inclusion' %}
                    <th class="inferred-header {{ group.header_class | default('') }} ontop-header" colspan="{{ group.fields|length }}">
                        <div>
                            <span>{{ group.friendly_name }}</span>
                            {#<!-- FIX: Restored original descriptive tooltip logic -->#}
                            <input type="checkbox" class="inclusion-checkbox" data-filter-group="{{ group.name }}" title="Enable to include (or limit to if it's the only checkbox enabled) papers about {{ group.friendly_name | default('other', true) | lower }}">
                        </div>
                    </th>
                    {% elif group.filter_type == 'none' %}
                        <th class="inferred-header {{ group.header_class | default('') }} ontop-header" colspan="{{ group.fields|length }}">
                            {{ group.friendly_name }}
                        </th>
                    {% endif %}
                {% endfor %}
                
                <th style="display:none" colspan="5"></th>
            </tr>

            {#<!-- ROW 4: Actual Column Headers (Sortable) -->#}
            <tr>
                {#<!-- 1. Leading Fixed -->#}
                <th class="rotate-header status-header editable-header" data-sort="pdf-link"><div><span>PDF</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th data-sort="title">Title (Click for article) <span class="sort-indicator"></span> </th>
                <th data-sort="year">Year <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="page_count" ><div><span>Pages</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th data-sort="journal">Journal/Conf <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header status-header editable-header" data-sort="type"><div><span>Type</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>

                {#<!-- 2. Universal Inferred -->#}
                <th class="inferred-header rotate-header status-header editable-header" data-sort="is_offtopic"><span class="sort-indicator"></span><div><span>Off-topic</span></div></th>
                <th class="inferred-header rotate-header status-header" data-sort="relevance"><span class="sort-indicator"></span><div><span>Relevance</span></div></th>

                {#<!-- 3. Dynamic Domain-Specific -->#}
                {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
                    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                        <th class="inferred-header {{ group.header_class | default('') }} rotate-header status-header editable-header" data-sort="{{ group.json_path }}">
                            <span class="sort-indicator"></span><div><span>{{ group.label }}</span></div>
                        </th>
                    {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                        {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                            <th class="inferred-header {{ group.header_class | default('') }} rotate-header status-header editable-header" data-sort="{{ group.json_path }}.{{ field_def.key }}">
                                <span class="sort-indicator"></span><div><span>{{ field_def.label }}</span></div>
                            </th>
                        {% endfor %}
                    {% endif %}
                {% endfor %}

                {#<!-- 4. Trailing Fixed -->#}
                <th data-sort="changed">Last Changed <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="changed_by"><div><span>Changed By</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="verified"><div><span>Verified</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="estimated_score"><div><span>Accr. Score</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="verified_by"><div><span>Verified By</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="user_override_count"><div><span>User Changes</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header status-header" data-sort="user_comment_state"><span class="sort-indicator"></span><div><span>Commented</span></div></th>
                <th class="rotate-header"><div>History</div></th>
                <th class="rotate-header"><div>Details</div></th>

                {#<!-- 5. Hidden Metadata -->#}
                <th style="display:none" colspan="5"></th>
            </tr>
        </thead>

            <tbody>
                <tr>
                    <td style="padding:0;" colspan="{{ total_cols }}">
                        <div class="loading-overlay">
                            <div class="header-branding"><span>Research</span><span style="font-weight: 600; color: var(--primary-ui-color);">Parsa</span></div>
                            <div class="loading-spinner"></div>
                            <div>Reprocessing table. Wait patiently. </div>
                        </div>
                    </td>
                    <td style="display:none" colspan="5"></td>
                </tr>
            </tbody>   
            
            {% include 'shared/papers_table_tfoot.html' %}
            {#<!-- Use the rendered table content passed from the server -->#} 
    </table>
</div>

<div id="batchModal" class="modal">
    <div id="batch-actions">
        <div>
            <button class="action-btn" id="screen-remaining-btn">Quick <strong>Screening</strong></button>
        </div>
        <div>
            <button class="action-btn" id="classify-consensus-btn">Automated Classify <strong>Until Consensus</strong></button>
        </div>
        <div>
            <button class="action-btn" id="classify-remaining-btn">Classify <strong>Remaining</strong> </button>
            {#<!--<!-- <button class="action-btn" id="classify-impl-btn" disabled>Re-Classify <strong>Primary</strong></button>
        </div>
        <div>
            <button class="action-btn" id="classify-misclassified-btn" disabled>Re-Classify <strong>Suspects</strong> </button> -->-->#}
            <button class="action-btn" id="classify-all-btn">Re-Classify <strong>All</strong></button>
        </div>
        <span class="menu-message">Batch classifications also run corresponding verifications automatically. Run the tasks below if you still need just verification (e.g., if an earlier run was aborted after classification)</span>
        <div>
            <button class="action-btn" id="verify-remaining-btn">Verify <strong>Remaining</strong></button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="verify-all-btn">Re-Verify <strong>All</strong></button>
        </div>
        <span class="menu-message" id="batch-status-message">These tools stay running in background after being called. Reload the webpage after a while to see updated results.
            <br><br> For actions on single papers, user the buttons in the corresponding paper's detail pane instead.
            <br><br> You may check the agreement report below after the complete dataset is classified to consensus to assess the classification consistency and surface possible problems.
        </span> 
        <div style="width: 100%; text-align: center;">
            <a id="agreement-report-btn" href="/agreement_report" target="_blank"
            title="Open the whole-dataset 3-run agreement report in a new tab">
                📊 3-Run <strong>Agreement Report</strong> 
            </a>
            <a id="queue-logs-btn" href="/queue_logs" target="_blank"
            title="Open the queue manager log viewer in a new tab">
                📜 Queue Manager <strong>Log Viewer</strong> 
            </a>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<div id="importModal" class="modal">
    <div id="import-actions">
        {#<!-- unused, kept if a menu is desired here later (animations, etc. already done) -->#}
    </div>
</div>

<div id="exportModal" class="modal">
    <div id="export-actions">
        <div>
            <button class="action-btn" id="export-html-btn">Export <strong>HTML</strong></button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="export-xlsx-btn">Export <strong>XLSX</strong></button>            

            <label style="font-size: 10pt; padding: 12px 0 0 6px; display: inline-block;">
                <input type="checkbox" id="lite-export-checkbox" checked>
                <span style="padding: 0 0 0 8px">Lite export (no thinking traces)</span>
            </label>

            <label style="font-size: 10pt; padding: 4px 0 0 6px; display: inline-block;">
                <input type="checkbox" id="skip-abstracts-checkbox">
                <span style="padding: 0 0 0 8px">Skip abstracts</span>
            </label>

        </div>        
        <div>
            <button class="action-btn" id="backup-btn" > <strong>Create </strong>Backup</button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="restore-btn" > <strong>Restore </strong>Backup</button>
        </div>
        <span class="menu-message" id="backup-status-message">Backups include the database, original and annotated PDFs, HTML export and a XLSX spreadsheet.
            <br><br>Restoring from a backup overwrites all existing data!
        </span>
    </div>
</div>

<div id="statsModal" class="modal">
    <div class="modal-content">
        <span class="close">&times;</span>
        {% include 'shared/stats_modal_content.html' %}
    </div>
</div>

<div id="aboutModal" class="modal">
    <div class="modal-small-content">
        <span class="close">&times;</span>      
        {% include 'shared/about_modal_content.html' %}
    </div>
</div>
</body>
</html>
```

# web\templates\shared\detail_form.html
```html
{#<!-- shared/detail_form.html -->#}
{% macro render_detail_form(paper, domain_config, editable=true) %}
<form id="form-{{ paper.id }}" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">
  {% for field in domain_config.editable_fields %}
    <label>{{ field.label }}:
      <input type="text" class="editable" name="{{ field.json_path }}"
             value="{{ paper.classification | get_json_path(field.json_path) or '' }}"
             {{ '' if editable else 'disabled' }}>
    </label>
  {% endfor %}
  <label>Page count:
    <input type="text" class="editable" name="page_count"
           value="{{ paper.page_count or '' }}" {{ '' if editable else 'disabled' }}>
  </label>
  <label>Relevance:
    <input type="text" class="editable" name="relevance"
           value="{{ paper.classification | get_json_path('relevance') or '' }}"
           placeholder="0 - 10" {{ '' if editable else 'disabled' }}>
  </label>
  <label>User comments:
    <textarea class="editable" name="user_trace" {{ '' if editable else 'disabled' }}>{{ paper.user_trace or '' }}</textarea>
  </label>
  {% if editable %}
    <div class="row-actions">
      <div>
        <button type="button" class="action-btn classify-btn" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">Classify <strong>this paper</strong></button>
        <button type="button" class="action-btn verify-btn" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">Verify <strong>this paper</strong></button>
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="action-btn save-btn" title="Ctrl+S or click here to save changes"
              onclick="saveChanges('{{ paper.id }}')">Save Changes</button>
    </div>
  {% endif %}
</form>
{% endmacro %}
```

# web\templates\shared\history_table.html
```html
{#<!-- shared/history_table.html -->#}

{#<!-- Renders the trace/log-entry panel (left column of a history tab) -->#}
{% macro render_trace_panel(log_entries, set_num, is_lite_export=false) %}
<div class="trace-content">
    {% if log_entries %}
        {% for entry in log_entries %}
            <div class="log-entry log-type-{{ entry.type }}">
                <div class="log-header">
                    <span class="log-timestamp">{{ entry.timestamp|format_changed_timestamp }}</span>
                    <span class="log-type">{% if entry.type == 'trace_review' %}Agent Trace Review{% else %}{{ entry.type | capitalize }}{% endif %}{% if set_num %} (Set {{ set_num }}){% endif %}</span>
                    {% if entry.type != 'user' %}<span class="log-model">Model: {{ entry.model }}</span>{% endif %}
                    <span class="log-validity">
                        {% if entry.valid %}
                            <span class="valid-indicator valid">✓ Valid</span>
                        {% else %}
                            <span class="valid-indicator invalid" title="{{ entry.invalid_reason|default('') }}">✗ Invalid</span>
                            {% if entry.invalid_reason %}<span class="invalid-reason">{{ entry.invalid_reason }}</span>{% endif %}
                        {% endif %}
                    </span>
                </div>
                {% if entry.type == 'trace_review' %}
                    {#<!-- Free-form report: print the final answer, collapse the thinking trace -->#}
                    <div class="trace-review-report"><pre>{{ (entry.output or {}).get('report', '') }}</pre></div>
                    {% if entry.trace and not is_lite_export %}
                        <details class="trace-review-thinking">
                            <summary>Show thinking trace</summary>
                            <div class="log-trace"><pre>{{ entry.trace }}</pre></div>
                        </details>
                    {% endif %}
                {% else %}
                    {% if entry.trace %}
                        <div class="log-trace"><pre>{% if is_lite_export %}Thinking trace unavailable in lite export{% else %}{{ entry.trace }}{% endif %}</pre></div>
                    {% endif %}
                    {% if entry.verification_data %}
                        <div class="log-verification-summary">
                            <strong>Verification:</strong>
                            {% if entry.verification_data.verified == 1 %}✓ Approved
                            {% elif entry.verification_data.verified == 0 %}✗ Rejected
                            {% else %}❔ Unknown{% endif %}
                            {% if entry.verification_data.estimated_score is not none %} | Score: {{ entry.verification_data.estimated_score }}{% endif %}
                        </div>
                    {% endif %}
                {% endif %}
            </div>
        {% endfor %}
    {% else %}
        <div class="log-entry log-type-user"><p>No classification data{% if set_num %} for Set {{ set_num }}{% endif %}.</p></div>
    {% endif %}
</div>
{% endmacro %}


{#<!-- Renders the history table (right column of a history tab) -->#}
{% macro render_history_table(log_entries, domain_config, show_certainty=false, show_actions=false, paper_id='') %}
<div id="history-table-wrapper">
  <table class="history-table">
    <colgroup>
      <col style="width: 27px;"><col style="width: 27px;">
      {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
        {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}<col style="width: 27px;">
        {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
          {% for f in group.fields %}<col style="width: 27px;">{% endfor %}
        {% endif %}
      {% endfor %}
      <col style="width: 56px;"><col style="width: 40px;"><col style="width: 27px;">
      <col style="width: 32px;"><col style="width: 27px;">
    </colgroup>
    <tbody>
      {% for entry in log_entries %}
        {% if entry.type in ['classifier', 'consensus', 'averaged_llm', 'user', 'screener'] and entry.valid %}
          {% set data = entry.output %}
          {% set changed = entry.changed_fields %}
          {% set cert = entry.certainty_map if show_certainty and entry.certainty_map else {} %}
          <tr>
            <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ cert.get('is_offtopic', 'solid') }}{% if 'is_offtopic' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
              {% if cert.get('is_offtopic') == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
              {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ data.is_offtopic | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
            </td>
            <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell{% if 'relevance' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
              {% if data.relevance is defined and data.relevance is not none %}{{ (data.relevance|float|round(1)|string).rstrip('0').rstrip('.') }}{% endif %}
            </td>
            {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
              {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                {% set path = group.json_path %}
                <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ cert.get(path, 'solid') }}{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                  {% if cert.get(path) == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
                  {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ data | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
                </td>
              {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                  {% set path = group.json_path + '.' + field_def.key %}
                  {% if field_def.get('render_type') == 'text_presence' %}
                    <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ cert.get(path, 'solid') }}{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                      {% set text_val = data | get_json_path(path) %}
                      <span class="emoji-content">{{ '✔️' if text_val and (text_val|string).strip() else '❌' }}</span>
                    </td>
                  {% else %}
                    <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ cert.get(path, 'solid') }}{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                      {% if cert.get(path) == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
                      {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ data | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
                    </td>
                  {% endif %}
                {% endfor %}
              {% endif %}
            {% endfor %}
            <td class="secondary-text-cell changed-cell">{{ entry.timestamp|format_changed_timestamp }}</td>
            <td class="status-cell changed-by-cell">{{ entry.model|render_changed_by }}</td>
              {% if entry.type in ['averaged_llm', 'screener'] %}
              <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ cert.get('verified', 'solid') }}{% if 'verified' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
              {% if cert.get('verified') == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
              {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ data.verified|default(None)|render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
              </td>
              <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell{% if 'estimated_score' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
              {% if data.estimated_score is not none %}{{ data.estimated_score }}{% endif %}
              </td>
              <td class="status-cell">{% if data.verified is not none %}🖥️{% else %}❔{% endif %}</td>
              {% elif entry.type == 'user' %}
              <td class="status-cell{% if 'verified' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                <span class="emoji-content">{{ data.verified|default(None)|render_status }}</span>
              </td>
              <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell{% if 'estimated_score' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                {% if data.estimated_score is not none %}{{ data.estimated_score }}{% endif %}
              </td>
              <td class="status-cell">{{ data.verified_by|default('')|render_verified_by }}</td>
            {% elif entry.verification_data %}
              <td class="status-cell{% if 'verified' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                {{ entry.verification_data.verified|render_status }}
              </td>
              <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell{% if 'estimated_score' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                {% if entry.verification_data.estimated_score is not none %}{{ entry.verification_data.estimated_score }}{% endif %}
              </td>
              <td class="status-cell">{{ entry.verification_data.verifier_model|render_verified_by }}</td>
            {% else %}
              <td class="status-cell">❔</td>
              <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell"></td>
              <td class="status-cell">❔</td>
            {% endif %}
          </tr>
        {% endif %}
      {% endfor %}
    </tbody>
  </table>
</div>
{% if show_actions %}
  <div class="history-info">
    <div style="text-align:center">
      <span>History sorted from <b>newest</b> to <b>oldest</b>.</span><br><br>
      <span>Highlights identify cells changed between runs.</span><br>
      <span>⚠️ indicates conflicting classifications across the 3 sets.</span><br>
      <span>Translucent emojis indicate partial agreement (33% = 1 set, 66% = 2 sets agree).</span>
    </div>
  </div>
<div class="row-actions">    
  <div>
    <button type="button" class="action-btn trace-review-btn" data-paper-id="{{ paper_id }}" title="Send the full 3-set log stream to the LLM for a free-form meta-review. Does not change the classification.">
      Auto <strong>Audit</strong> traces</button>
  </div>
  <div>
    <button type="button" class="action-btn classify-btn" data-paper-id="{{ paper_id }}">Classify <strong>this paper</strong></button>
    <button type="button" class="action-btn verify-btn" data-paper-id="{{ paper_id }}">Verify <strong>this paper</strong></button>
  </div>
</div>
{% endif %}
{% endmacro %}


{#<!-- Full tab panel: trace + table, used 4× per paper -->#}
{% macro render_history_tab(paper, set_num, tab_name, domain_config, show_certainty=false, show_actions=false, is_lite_export=false) %}
<div class="history-tab-panel {% if tab_name == 'main' %}active{% endif %}" data-tab-panel="{{ tab_name }}" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">
  {% set log_entries = paper['llm_log_entries'] if set_num is none else paper['set_' + (set_num|string) + '_llm_log_entries'] %}
  {{ render_trace_panel(log_entries, set_num, is_lite_export) }}
  {{ render_history_table(log_entries, domain_config, show_certainty, show_actions, paper.id) }}
</div>
{% endmacro %}
```

# web\templates\shared\papers_table_tfoot.html
```html
{#<!-- templates/papers_table_tfoot.html -->#}
{% set ns = namespace(total_dynamic=0) %}
{% for group in domain_config.groups %}
    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
        {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + 1 %}
    {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
        {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + (group.fields | length) %}
    {% endif %}
{% endfor %}
{% set total_cols = 6 + 2 + ns.total_dynamic + 9 %}

<tfoot id="counts-footer">
<tr>
    <td id="count-pdf_present" title=""></td> 
    <td colspan="4" style="font-weight: 400;" id="visible-count-cell"> 
        Filtered: <span style="font-weight: bold;" id="visible-papers-count"></span>,
        Loaded: <span style="font-weight: bold;" id="loaded-papers-count"></span>,
        Total: <span style="font-weight: bold;" id="total-papers-count">{{ total_paper_count | safe }}</span>,
        Duplicate: <span style="font-weight: bold;" id="duplicate-papers-count"></span>
    </td>
    <td>
        <span style="font-size:0.75em;padding-left: 30px;position: absolute; left: 2px;bottom: 3px;">
            <button type="button" id="longtable-btn" title="Click to copy the currently filtered table to the clipboard as a LaTeX \longtable"><em>\longtable</em></button>
        </span>
    </td>
    
    {#<!-- Universal Inferred Counts -->#}
    <td data-count-field="is_offtopic" class="count-cell" title="Offtopic"></td>
    <td></td> {#<!-- Relevance -->#}

    {#<!-- Dynamic Domain-Specific Counts -->#}
    {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
        {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
            <td data-count-field="{{ group.json_path }}" class="count-cell" title="{{ group.label }}"></td>
        {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
            {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                <td data-count-field="{{ group.json_path }}.{{ field_def.key }}" class="count-cell" title="{{ field_def.label }}"></td>
            {% endfor %}
        {% endif %}
    {% endfor %}

    {#<!-- Trailing Fixed Counts -->#}
    <td></td> {#<!-- Last Changed -->#}
    <td data-count-field="changed_by" class="count-cell" title="Changed by User"></td>
    <td data-count-field="verified" class="count-cell" title="Verified"></td>
    <td></td> {#<!-- Estimated Score -->#}
    <td data-count-field="verified_by" class="count-cell" title="Verified by User"></td>
    <td data-count-field="user_override_count" class="count-cell" title="User Override Count"></td>
    <td data-count-field="user_comment_state" class="count-cell" title="Commented by User"></td>
    <td></td> {#<!-- History -->#}
    <td></td> {#<!-- Actions -->#}
    
    {#<!-- Hidden metadata fields -->#}
    <td style="display:none" colspan="5"></td>
</tr>
</tfoot>
```

# web\templates\shared\stats_modal_content.html
```html
{#<!-- templates/stats_modal_content.html -->#}
{#<!-- <p> Data in <strong>all lists</strong> below is dynamically calculated based on <strong>currently visible</strong> papers:</p> -->#}
<div class="stats-lists">
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3>Repeating <strong>Journals</strong> <span style="font-size:0.75em">{#<!-- — Scroll down on each list for more-->#} ↓</span></h3>
        <ul id="journalStatsList" class="stats-list-multicolumn-2"></ul>
    </div>
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3>Repeating <strong>Conferences</strong> <span style="font-size:0.75em">↓</span>
            <span style="font-size:0.75em;padding-left: 30px;position: absolute; right: 1px;top: 5px;">
                <button type="button" class="tabularx-btn" id="journalconf-tabularx-btn" title="Click to copy a LaTeX table with this data to the clipboard"><em>\tabularx</em></button>
            </span> 
        </h3>
        <ul id="conferenceStatsList" class="stats-list-multicolumn-2"></ul> 
    </div>
    <div class="stats-section" style="grid-column: 1/3;">
        <h3>Repeating <strong>Keywords</strong> <span style="font-size:0.75em">↓</span>
            <span style="font-size:0.75em;padding-left: 30px;position: absolute; right: 20px;top: 11px;">
                <label style="margin-left:10px;">
                    Cloud <input checked="" type="checkbox" id="cloudToggle" style="transform: scale(2) translate(5px,-0px);">
                </label>
            </span> 
        </h3>
        <ul id="keywordStatsList" class="stats-list-multicolumn-short"></ul>     
        <div id="keywordCloudCanvas"
            style="display: block; margin: auto; width: 100%; height: 285px;box-sizing: border-box;">
            {#<!-- <canvas width="640" height="280"></canvas> -->#}
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3>Repeating <strong>Authors</strong> <span style="font-size:0.75em">↓</span>
            <span style="font-size:0.75em;padding-left: 30px;position: absolute; right: 1px;top: 5px;">
                <button type="button" class="tabularx-btn" id="authors-tabularx-btn" title="Click to copy a LaTeX table with this data to the clipboard"><em>\tabularx</em></button>
            </span> 
        </h3>
        <ul id="authorStatsList" class="stats-list-multicolumn-2"></ul>
    </div>
    <div class="stats-section" style="display: none;">
        <h3>Repeating <strong>Research Areas</strong> <span style="font-size:0.75em">↓</span></h3>
        <ul id="researchAreaStatsList" class="stats-list-multicolumn-2"></ul>
    </div>

    
{% set ns = namespace(slot1=null, slot2=null, count=0) %}
{% for field in domain_config.editable_fields %}
    {% if field.stats_list %}
        {% if ns.count == 0 %}
            {% set ns.slot1 = field %}
            {% set ns.count = 1 %}
        {% elif ns.count == 1 %}
            {% set ns.slot2 = field %}
            {% set ns.count = 2 %}
        {% endif %}
    {% endif %}
{% endfor %}
```
