{#<!-- ... (Keep Journals, Conferences, Keywords, Authors, Research Areas, and Other Metrics exactly as they are) ... -->#}

{#<!-- SLOT 1 -->#}
<div class="stats-section">
    <h3>Mentioned <strong>{{ ns.slot1.label if ns.slot1 else '(Unconfigured)' }}</strong> <span style="font-size:0.75em">↓</span>
        {% if ns.slot1 %}
        <span style="font-size:0.75em;padding-left: 30px;position: absolute; right: 1px;top: 5px;">
            <button type="button" class="tabularx-btn" id="slot1-tabularx-btn" title="Click to copy a LaTeX table with this data to the clipboard"><em>\tabularx</em></button>
        </span>
        {% endif %}
    </h3>
    <ul id="slot1StatsList" class="stats-list-multicolumn-2"></ul>
</div>

{#<!-- SLOT 2 -->#}
<div class="stats-section">
    <h3>Mentioned <strong>{{ ns.slot2.label if ns.slot2 else '(Unconfigured)' }}</strong> <span style="font-size:0.75em">↓</span>
        {% if ns.slot2 %}
        <span style="font-size:0.75em;padding-left: 30px;position: absolute; right: 1px;top: 5px;">
            <button type="button" class="tabularx-btn" id="slot2-tabularx-btn" title="Click to copy a LaTeX table with this data to the clipboard"><em>\tabularx</em></button>
        </span>
        {% endif %}
    </h3>
    <ul id="slot2StatsList" class="stats-list-multicolumn-2"></ul>
</div>


    
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Other</strong> Metrics
            <span style="font-size:0.75em;padding-left: 30px;position: absolute; right: 1px;top: 5px;">
                <button type="button" class="tabularx-btn" id="metrics-tabularx-btn" title="Click to copy a LaTeX table with this data to the clipboard"><em>\tabularx</em></button>
            </span> 
        </h3>
        <table id="metricsTableStatsList" class="stats-table"></table>
    </div>
</div>
        
<div class="stats-charts">
    
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Publ. Type</strong> 

        </h3>
        <div class="chart-container" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="publTypePieChart"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Survey</strong> vs <strong>Primary</strong></h3>
        <div class="chart-container" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="surveyVsImplPieChart"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Techniques</strong> Distribution 
            <span style="font-size:0.75em;padding-left: 30px;position: absolute; right: 6px;top: 11px; 
            display: inline;">
                <label style="padding: 10px;margin: 10px;">
                    Pie<input type="checkbox" id="pieToggle" style="transform: scale(2) translate(5px,-0px);">
                </label>
            </span>
        </h3>
        <div class="chart-container" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="techniquesPieChart"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Features</strong> Distribution
            <span style="font-size:0.75em;padding-left: 30px;position: absolute; right: 6px;top: 11px; 
            display: inline;">
                <label style="padding: 10px;margin: 10px;">
                    Group<input type="checkbox" id="featureGroupToggle" style="transform: scale(2) translate(5px,-0px);">
                </label>
            </span>
        </h3>
        <div class="chart-container" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="featuresPieChart"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>


    <div class="stats-section">                
        <h3><strong>Publication Types</strong> per Year

        </h3>
        <div class="chart-container-fullwidth" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="pubTypesPerYearLineChart"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Survey</strong> vs <strong>Primary</strong> Papers per Year  

        </h3>
        <div class="chart-container-fullwidth" style="height:calc(100% - 34px);  margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="surveyVsImplLineChart"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Techniques</strong> per Year             
            <span style="font-size:0.75em;padding-left: 30px;position: absolute;right: 40px;top: 11px;
            display: inline;">
                <label>
                    Cumulative<input type="checkbox" id="cumulativeToggle" style="transform: scale(2) translate(5px,-0px);">
                </label>
                <label style="padding: 0 20px;margin: 10px;">
                    Stacked<input type="checkbox" id="stackingToggle" style="transform: scale(2) translate(5px,-0px);">
                </label>
            </span>
            <span style="font-size:0.75em;
            display: inline;"> — Click on a label to show/hide its data and reflow the chart </span>
        </h3>
        <div class="chart-container-fullwidth" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="techniquesPerYearLineChart"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Features</strong> per Year         
            <span style="font-size:0.75em;
            display: inline;"> — Data in this page is dynamically calculated based on <strong>currently visible</strong> papers.</span>
        </h3>
        <div class="chart-container-fullwidth" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="featuresPerYearLineChart"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Relevance Score</strong> Histogram </h3>
        <div class="chart-container-fullwidth" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="RelevanceHistogram"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Verifier Score</strong> Histogram </h3>
        <div class="chart-container-fullwidth" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="estScoreHistogram"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>SMT</strong> vs <strong>THT</strong> </h3>
        <div class="chart-container-fullwidth" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="SMTvsTHTPieChart"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="stats-section">
        <h3><strong>Dataset</strong> Scope </h3>
        <div class="chart-container-fullwidth" style="height:calc(100% - 34px); margin: auto; overflow: hidden;">
            <canvas id="OffTopicPieChart"></canvas>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

# web\templates\shared\table_colgroup.html

{#<!-- partials/table_colgroup.html -->#}
{% set ns = namespace(total_dynamic=0) %}
{% for group in domain_config.groups %}
  {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
    {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + 1 %}
  {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
    {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + (group.fields | length) %}
  {% endif %}
{% endfor %}
{% set inferred_colspan = 2 + ns.total_dynamic %}
{% set total_cols = 6 + inferred_colspan + 9 %}

<colgroup>
  {#<!-- 1. LEADING FIXED (6) -->#}
  <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
  <col style="width: 66%">
  <col style="width: 40px; min-width: 40px; max-width:40px;">
  <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
  <col style="width: 34%">
  <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
  {#<!-- 2. UNIVERSAL INFERRED (2) -->#}
  <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
  <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
  {#<!-- 3. DYNAMIC DOMAIN-SPECIFIC -->#}
  {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
      <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
    {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
      {% for field_def in group.fields %}
        <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
      {% endfor %}
    {% endif %}
  {% endfor %}
  {#<!-- 4. TRAILING FIXED (9) -->#}
  <col style="width: 56px; min-width: 56px; max-width: 56px;">
  <col style="width: 40px; min-width: 40px; max-width: 40px;">
  <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
  <col style="width: 32px; min-width: 32px; max-width: 32px;">
  <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
  <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
  <col style="width: 27px; min-width: 27px; max-width: 27px;">
  <col style="width: 44px; min-width: 44px; max-width: 44px;">
  <col style="width: 44px; min-width: 44px; max-width: 44px;">
  {#<!-- 5. HIDDEN METADATA (5) -->#}
  <col style="display:none"><col style="display:none"><col style="display:none">
  <col style="display:none"><col style="display:none">
</colgroup>

# web\templates\static_export\loader.html
```html
<!-- templates/loader.html -->
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>Loading ResearchParsa...</title>    
    <link href="data:image/x-icon;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAABAAAAAQEAYAAABPYyMiAAAABmJLR0T///////8JWPfcAAAACXBIWXMAAABIAAAASABGyWs+AAAAF0lEQVRIx2NgGAWjYBSMglEwCkbBSAcACBAAAeaR9cIAAAAASUVORK5CYII=" rel="icon" type="image/x-icon" />

    <style>
        /* Minimal styling for the loading indicator */
        body { font-family: sans-serif; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 40px; justify-content: center; align-items: center; height: 100vh; margin: 0; background-color: #f5f5f5; }
        .loading, #previewMessage { text-align: center; }
        /* .spinner { border: 4px solid #f3f3f3; border-top: 4px solid #3498db; border-radius: 50%; width: 30px; height: 30px; animation: spin 1s linear infinite; margin: 0 auto 10px; } */    
        .spinner {
            width: 60px;
            height: 60px;
            border: 12px solid hsl(188, 36%, 30%);
            border-top: 12px solid transparent;
            border-radius: 50%;
            animation: spin 0.75s linear infinite;
            margin: 0 auto 30px; 
        }
        .header-branding{
            font-size: 48pt;
            justify-self: center;
            font-family: "Arial Narrow";
            font-weight: 400;
            color: #3f8d82;
            text-shadow: 1px 0 5px #fff6;
        }
        @keyframes spin { 0% { transform: rotate(0deg); } 100% { transform: rotate(360deg); } }
    </style>
    <!-- Inline the Pako library for Gzip decompression -->
    <script>
    {{ pako_js_content | safe }} 
    </script>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="header-branding"><span>Research</span><span style="font-weight: 600; color: hsl(188, 31%, 29%);">Parsa</span></div>
    <div class="loading" id="splash-spinner">
        <div class="spinner"></div>
        <p>Trying to decompress and load HTML static export...</p>
        <p>This file requires JavaScript to decompress and load its content.<br>
        Download and open it in a full (computer) web browser to view.</p>
        <!-- Warning apparently not needed anymore in latest Edge/Chromium -->
        <!-- <p><strong>This application has known performance issues in<br>
            Google Chrome / MS Edge.</strong>
        <p>Please use Mozilla Firefox for the best experience. -->
        </p>
    </div>
    <script>
        const compressedDataBase64 = "{{ compressed_html_data | safe }}"; // The compressed HTML data passed from Flask

        function loadContent() {
            try {
                const compressedBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(compressedDataBase64), c => c.charCodeAt(0));  // 1. Decode Base64 to get compressed bytes (as Uint8Array)
                const decompressedBytes = pako.inflate(compressedBytes);                                    // 2. Decompress using Pako (inflate)                
                const decompressedHtmlString = new TextDecoder("utf-8").decode(decompressedBytes);          // 3. Decode bytes back to string (assuming UTF-8)

                // 4. Write the full HTML content to the document, replacing this loader
                // Using document.write after the page loads replaces the entire document
                document.open();
                document.write(decompressedHtmlString);
                document.close();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Failed to decompress or load content:", error);
                document.body.innerHTML = "<p style='color:red;'>Error loading data. Please check the console for details.</p>";
            }
        }

        // Start loading when the window loads
        window.addEventListener('load', loadContent);
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

# web\templates\static_export\index_static_export.html
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en" class="busyCursor">
<head>
    <title>HTML Export - ResearchParsa - {{ domain_config.domain_name }}</title>
    <link rel="icon" href="data:image/svg+xml,<svg xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22 viewBox=%220 0 100 100%22><text y=%22.9em%22 font-size=%2290%22>📊</text></svg>">
    <style>
        {{ style_css_content | safe }}
        {#/* Disable animations for server-side elements*/#}
        .editable-status:active,.editable-status:hover,.editable-verify:active,.editable-verify:hover{transform:unset;background-color:unset;cursor:default}
        .editable-status:hover,.editable-verify:hover{box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.1)}
        .header-filters > div:last-child { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px; }
    </style>
    <script>
        window.APP_CONFIG = {{ domain_config | tojson | safe }};
        {{ chart_js_content | safe }}
        {{ chart_js_datalabels_content | safe }}
        {{ d3_js_content | safe }}
        {{ d3_cloud_js_content | safe }}
    </script>
</head>
<body id="html-export">
{% set ns = namespace(total_dynamic=0) %}
{% for group in domain_config.groups %}
    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
        {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + 1 %}
    {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
        {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + (group.fields | length) %}
    {% endif %}
{% endfor %}
{% set inferred_colspan = 2 + ns.total_dynamic %}
{% set total_cols = 6 + inferred_colspan + 9 %}

<div class="table-container" id="papers-table-container">
    <table id="papersTable" style="table-layout: fixed;">
        {% include 'shared/table_colgroup.html' %}
        <thead>
            <tr>
                <th id="main-header-wrapper" colspan="{{ total_cols }}" style="display: none;"></th>
                <th style="display:none" colspan="5"></th>
            </tr>
            <tr>
                <th class="ontop-header" colspan="6" rowspan="2">
                    <div class="header-filters">
                        <div class="filter-item">
                            <input type="checkbox" id="hide-short-checkbox" style="display: none;">
                            <label for="min-page-count">Min. page count ≥</label>
                            <input type="number" id="min-page-count" value="{{ min_page_count_value }}" min="{{ min_page_count_value }}" max="9" title="Exported file contains papers with at least {{ min_page_count_value }} pages">
                        </div>
                        <div class="filter-item">
                            <label for="year-from">Year:</label>
                            <input type="number" id="year-from" value="{{ year_from_value }}" min="{{ year_from_value }}" max="{{ year_to_value }}" title="Exported file contains papers from {{ year_from_value }} to {{ year_to_value }}.">
                            <label for="year-to">to</label>
                            <input type="number" id="year-to" value="{{ year_to_value }}" min="{{ year_from_value }}" max="{{ year_to_value }}" title="Exported file contains papers from {{ year_from_value }} to {{ year_to_value }}.">
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <button class="action-btn" id="about-btn" title="(F1) About / Help"><span>?</span></button>
                            <button class="action-btn" id="stats-btn">View <strong>Statistics</strong></button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </th>
                <th class="inferred-header" id="inferred-header-title" colspan="{{ inferred_colspan }}">
                    <div>
                        <span>Inferred data</span>
                        <div style="font-size: 7pt; font-family: 'Arial Narrow'; text-shadow: var(--light-shadow); transform:scale(2.2) translateY(-1px);">
                            <span style="font-weight: 400; opacity: 0.7;">Research</span><span style="font-weight: 600; opacity: 0.85;">Parsa</span> 
                        </div>                            
                        <div style="margin:0; padding:0">
                            <!-- Potential Misclassifications: -->
                            <input type="checkbox" title="Enable to only show papers marked as misclassified by the verifier" id="hide-approved-checkbox">
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </th>
                <th class="ontop-header" colspan="9" rowspan="2">
                    <div class="search-container">
                        <input type="search" id="search-input" placeholder="(F3) Type to search all fields..." value="">
                        <button type="button" id="clear-search-btn" class="clear-btn" title="Clear search">×</button>
                    </div>
                </th>
                <th style="display:none" colspan="5"></th>
            </tr>
            <tr>
                <th class="inferred-header ontop-header" colspan="1">
                    <div>
                        <input type="checkbox" id="hide-offtopic-checkbox" {% if hide_offtopic == true %}checked disabled title="Can't unhide offtopic papers. File was exported only with ontopic papers"{% endif %}>
                    </div>
                </th>
                <th class="inferred-header ontop-header" colspan="1"></th>
                
                {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
                    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                        <th class="inferred-header {{ group.header_class | default('') }} ontop-header" colspan="1">
                            <div>
                                <input type="checkbox" class="tri-state-checkbox" data-filter-group="{{ group.name }}" title="Currently showing all papers. Click to show only {{ group.label }}.">
                            </div>
                        </th>
                    {% elif group.filter_type == 'inclusion' %}
                        <th class="inferred-header {{ group.header_class | default('') }} ontop-header" colspan="{{ group.fields|length }}">
                            <div>
                                <span>{{ group.friendly_name }}</span>
                                <input type="checkbox" class="inclusion-checkbox" data-filter-group="{{ group.name }}" title="Enable to include (or limit to if it's the only checkbox enabled) papers about {{ group.friendly_name | default('other', true) | lower }} features.">
                            </div>
                        </th>
                    {% elif group.filter_type == 'none' %}
                        <th class="inferred-header {{ group.header_class | default('') }} ontop-header" colspan="{{ group.fields|length }}">
                            {{ group.friendly_name }}
                        </th>
                    {% endif %}
                {% endfor %}

                <th style="display:none" colspan="5"></th>
            </tr>
            <tr>
                <th class="rotate-header status-header editable-header" data-sort="pdf-link"><div><span>PDF</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th data-sort="title">Title (Click for article) <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th data-sort="year">Year <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="page_count"><div><span>Pages</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th data-sort="journal">Journal/Conf <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>    
                <th class="rotate-header status-header editable-header" data-sort="type"><div><span>Type</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                
                <th class="inferred-header rotate-header status-header editable-header" data-sort="is_offtopic"><span class="sort-indicator"></span><div><span>Off-topic</span></div></th>
                <th class="inferred-header rotate-header status-header" data-sort="relevance"><span class="sort-indicator"></span><div><span>Relevance</span></div></th>
                
                {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
                    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                        <th class="inferred-header {{ group.header_class | default('') }} rotate-header status-header editable-header" data-sort="{{ group.json_path }}">
                            <span class="sort-indicator"></span><div><span>{{ group.label }}</span></div>
                        </th>
                    {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                        {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                            <th class="inferred-header {{ group.header_class | default('') }} rotate-header status-header editable-header" data-sort="{{ group.json_path }}.{{ field_def.key }}">
                                <span class="sort-indicator"></span><div><span>{{ field_def.label }}</span></div>
                            </th>
                        {% endfor %}
                    {% endif %}
                {% endfor %}

                <th data-sort="changed">Last Changed <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="changed_by"><div><span>Changed By</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="verified"><div><span>Verified</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="estimated_score"><div><span>Accr. Score</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="verified_by"><div><span>Verified By</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header" data-sort="user_override_count"><div><span>User Changes</span></div> <span class="sort-indicator"></span></th>
                <th class="rotate-header status-header" data-sort="user_comment_state"><span class="sort-indicator"></span><div><span>Commented</span></div></th>
                <th class="rotate-header"><div>History</div></th>
                <th class="rotate-header"><div>Details</div></th>

                <th style="display:none" colspan="5"></th>
            </tr>
        </thead>
        
        <tbody>
            <tr>
                <td style="padding:0;" colspan="{{ total_cols }}">
                    <div class="loading-overlay">
                        <div class="header-branding"><span>Research</span><span style="font-weight: 600; color: var(--primary-ui-color);">Parsa</span></div>
                        <div class="loading-spinner"></div>
                        <div>Decompressing and loading data…</div>
                    </div>
                </td>
                <td style="display:none" colspan="5"></td>
            </tr>
        </tbody>
        
        {% include 'shared/papers_table_tfoot.html' %}
    </table>
</div>

<div id="statsModal" class="modal"><div class="modal-content"><span class="close">&times;</span>{% include 'shared/stats_modal_content.html' %}</div></div>
<div id="aboutModal" class="modal"><div class="modal-small-content"><span class="close">&times;</span>{% include 'shared/about_modal_content.html' %}</div></div>

<script>
    window.__PARSA_DATA__ = {{ papers_json | safe }};
    {{ core_js | safe }}
    {{ export_renderers_js | safe }}
    {{ stats_core_js | safe }}
    {{ stats_generic_js | safe }}
    {{ stats_charts_js | safe }}
    {{ stats_domain_js | safe }}
    {{ stats_latex_js | safe }}
    {{ filtering_js | safe }}
    {{ ghpages_js | safe }}
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
